On CLAUDE.md §5-A rule 2 ("never `USING (true)` on sensitive tables"): the vision/mission/values are the university's published institutional identity (they appear on the public website) and the stated foundation every strategic goal cascades from, not per-person or per-unit data; the policy is still restricted `TO authenticated`, so this is not public exposure -- flagged here rather than buried, since it is the first deliberate `USING (true)` in this schema. Two follow-on fixes found while verifying, not assumed: `StrategicValueRow` rendered its edit/delete icons unconditionally (harmless while only three roles could reach the page, wrong once everyone can) -- now gated on a new `canEdit` prop; and the page subtitle still read "(مدير الاستراتيجية فقط)" / "(Strategy Admin only)", which became actively misleading for the new majority audience -- removed from both locales. Verified end-to-end with a real temporary `employee`-role test account holding NO `strategicPlanning` grant, at both the RLS and browser layers: through the raw authenticated client it read the real vision and all 5 real `strategic_values` rows, while `INSERT` was rejected (`42501`) and `UPDATE` affected 0 rows -- proving the read opened without the write gate loosening; then live in the browser, the same account saw the "الرؤية والرسالة والقيم" tab, the real vision/mission text in all 4 inputs (every one `disabled`), zero `.sru-icon-action` buttons, and the "للاطلاع فقط — لا تملك صلاحية التعديل" notice. All temporary test data (auth user, profile, `user_roles` row) deleted afterward scoped to the exact created ids, with the real `strategic_identity` row and all 5 real `strategic_values` reconfirmed intact. `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (144 tests -- 3 pre-existing `navItems.test.tsx` assertions updated, since they encoded the very behavior being fixed) all passed clean.

- **Strategic Plans list + per-plan detail page built and verified, closing the last explicit ask in this thread** (2026-08-01): the project owner asked for a dedicated page listing all `strategic_plans` rows with an add-plan button, where clicking a plan opens the existing four-section screen (بنك الأهداف / الأهداف المسندة / الأهداف الاستراتيجية / الرؤية والرسالة والقيم) with that plan's own name shown at the top ("بحيث أعلم أنا شغال على أي خطة") -- the Annual Plan mentioned in the same request is explicitly deferred, not started here. `/kpis/plans/page.tsx` (built schema-only by an earlier same-day PR, #117) was previously gated at `strategicPlanning>='view'`, a grant only 2-3 roles hold, making it an unreachable dead end for the sidebar's own landing link -- loosened to fully ungated (same "vision/mission opened to everyone" precedent, PR #118), with the create-plan form staying `approve`-gated; each row now links to a new `src/app/[locale]/(app)/kpis/plans/[id]/page.tsx`. That new per-plan page reproduces the four existing standalone screens (`kpis/strategic-identity`, `kpis/strategic-goals`, `kpis`, `goals/library`) as tabs via the already-established `ProfileTabs` client component -- `GroupTabs` (the group's normal top-tab-bar mechanism) can't render tabs for a dynamic route segment, so `ProfileTabs` was the natural reuse target, same as `/profile`/`/reports`. Vision/mission/values and the goal_library catalog are confirmed genuinely global (no `plan_id` column on either table) and shown unfiltered, exactly as their standalone pages already do; strategic goals/sub-goals/KPIs/targets are filtered to the plan's own goal ids in JS after the same RLS-scoped fetches the existing pages already use (small reference tables, same fetch-then-filter precedent already used for the cascaded-targets split on `/kpis`) -- `strategic_goals.plan_id` has been `NOT NULL` since 20260730000001, so this is a real, not cosmetic, filter.

- **Found and fixed a real RLS gap live during verification, not assumed**: `strategic_plans_select`'s existing policy (`check_vpra_global('strategicPlanning','view') OR EXISTS (goals for this plan)`) -- the EXISTS subquery is itself subject to `strategic_goals_select`'s own RLS, so it only counts goals the CALLER can already see; a real temporary plain-`employee` test account (no `strategicPlanning` grant, no position in the one real goal's cascade chain) saw "لا توجد خطط استراتيجية بعد" even though a real plan (`رؤية الجامعة 2030`, with a real linked goal) exists in production -- silently defeating the explicit request that clicking the sidebar always shows the plans list. Fixed with migration `20260801000001_strategic_plans_readable_by_all.sql`, widening `strategic_plans_select` to `USING (true)` `TO authenticated`, same category of change as the vision/mission fix (plan name/year-range is non-sensitive administrative metadata; the sensitive nested content keeps its own unchanged, much narrower RLS); write policies (`strategic_plans_insert`/`update`, `strategy_admin`-only) were untouched. Verified live end-to-end against the real shared database with two real temporary accounts: the plain employee, before the RLS fix, saw the plans list empty; after it, saw the real plan, opened its detail page, saw the plan's own Arabic/English name and year range at the top, and correctly saw all four tabs render (بنك الأهداف empty, الأهداف المسندة empty, الأهداف الاستراتيجية empty -- correctly excluded by `strategic_goals_select`'s own unchanged RLS, not a bug -- الرؤية والرسالة والقيم showing the real read-only vision/mission/values); a real temporary `strategy_admin` test account saw the same plan's detail page with a fully populated الأهداف الاستراتيجية tab (the real `تعزيز التميز المؤسسي` goal and its two real sub-goals, `مدير رأس المال البشري`/`النائب المساعد للتميز الأكاديمي`), plus the add-goal/manage-KPIs/add-sub-goal actions correctly shown only for that account. `navItems.test.tsx` updated for the new ungated `kpis/plans` child (six assertions across `strategicPlan` group tests). `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (144 tests) all passed clean. All temporary test data (2 auth users/profiles/user_roles rows) deleted afterward, confirmed zero residual rows; the one real production plan/goal/sub-goals were reconfirmed unchanged throughout.

- **Simplified the strategic-plan group's top tab bar to two tabs, reordered the per-plan detail page's tabs, and restored the sub-goal-level "إدارة المؤشرات" action** (2026-08-01), from direct feedback on the just-merged plans-list/per-plan-detail feature (PR #119). (1) The project owner pointed out that `/kpis/plans`' own top tab bar still showed "بنك الأهداف"/"الأهداف الاستراتيجية"/"الرؤية والرسالة والقيم" alongside "قائمة الخطط" -- redundant now that each of those three lives inside the per-plan detail page as its own tab ("لا تظهر العناوين الاخرى ... لأن لكل لها عناوينها الخاص بها فقط أظهر الأهداف المسندة"). Removed `kpis/strategic-identity`, `kpis/strategic-goals`, and `goals/library` from `navGroups.strategicPlan.children` in `navItems.ts` entirely -- the group now has exactly two children, `kpis/plans` and `kpis` (الأهداف المسندة), both already ungated, so this group's visibility no longer varies by permission at all. Their three standalone routes still exist and are still linked to directly from other flows (e.g. the manage-KPIs button, the goal-library page's own explanatory text) -- only the top-level tab bar entries were removed, not the pages themselves; `GroupTabs` on those three pages now simply shows the same two remaining tabs with neither highlighted, since `current` no longer matches anything in the (now-shorter) children list -- a harmless, accepted side effect, not fixed further since those pages are no longer meant to be reached via this tab bar. (2) Reordered the per-plan detail page's own four tabs (`/kpis/plans/[id]`) to match the exact sequence given directly: الرؤية والرسالة والقيم -> الأهداف الاستراتيجية -> الأهداف المسندة (**"سنعمل عليها لاحقا"** -- explicitly deferred, left unchanged) -> بنك الأهداف; vision/mission is now the default (first) tab shown on load. (3) Found and fixed a real content gap while implementing the reorder, confirmed against the attached screenshot: the sub-goals table inside "الأهداف الاستراتيجية" had silently lost its per-row "إدارة المؤشرات" (Manage Indicators) action link when this tab's content was originally adapted from the standalone `/kpis/strategic-goals` page (2026-08-01, PR #119) -- that adaptation copied the simpler read-only sub-goals table shape used by `/goals/library`'s quick-reference section instead of the original page's own full markup, which always had a per-sub-goal `/kpis/manage-kpis?subGoalId=...` link (the goal-level "إدارة المؤشرات"/"إضافة هدف فرعي" buttons were correctly preserved, only the sub-goal-level one was dropped). Restored it, gated behind the same `canManageGoals` check already used for the goal-level actions on this now-ungated page (a defensive addition beyond the original page's own behavior, which never needed this gate since that whole page was already `approve`-only). Verified live end-to-end with two real temporary accounts against the real shared database: a plain employee saw `/kpis/plans`' tab bar reduced to exactly "قائمة الخطط"/"الأهداف المسندة", and the per-plan detail page opened directly on "الرؤية والرسالة والقيم" with the correct tab order; a temporary `strategy_admin` account saw the same per-plan page's "الأهداف الاستراتيجية" tab with both the goal-level and, now correctly restored, both sub-goal-level "إدارة المؤشرات" links present and correctly linking to `/kpis/manage-kpis?subGoalId=...` for each of the plan's two real sub-goals. `navItems.test.tsx` updated for the two-child group (several tests consolidated/simplified since the group's visibility no longer varies by permission). `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (142 tests) all passed clean. All temporary test data (2 auth users/profiles/user_roles rows) deleted afterward, confirmed zero residual rows; the real production plan/goal/sub-goals reconfirmed unchanged throughout.

- **Restored two missing "تفريع مستهدف" (cascade target) action links on the per-plan detail page's الأهداف المسندة tab** (2026-08-01), found live during the requested "check the assigned goals tab too" verification of PRs #119/#120. Confirmed via a real temporary employee test account assigned to the real "مدير رأس المال البشري" position (which genuinely owns the real "تفعيل منظومة مدفوعة بالأداء" sub-goal): the standalone `/kpis` page correctly showed a "تفريع مستهدف" link on the owned sub-goal row, but the SAME data on the per-plan page's tab (`/kpis/plans/[id]`, built in PR #119) rendered the identical table with the action column and its `/kpis/assign?subGoalId=...` link entirely missing -- the exact same class of bug already found and fixed once for the sub-goal-level "إدارة المؤشرات" link in PR #120, this time in the "مؤشرات الأداء" (ownedSubGoals) table. While fixing it, re-diffed the ENTIRE assignedContent block against the original `/kpis/page.tsx` line by line rather than assuming a single missed spot, and found a second, not-yet-reported instance of the identical pattern: the "ما تملكه حاليًا" (ownedTargets) table was also missing its own cascade link (`/kpis/assign?parentTargetId=...`) -- the "المُسند إليك مباشرة" and "ما أسندته لمن دوني" tables were re-confirmed to correctly have NO action column in the original either, so no third fix was needed there. Both restored with an unconditional `<th />`/`<td>` column (matching the original page's own unconditional rendering -- unlike the sub-goal-level "إدارة المؤشرات" fix in PR #120, these two links were never gated behind `canManageGoals` on the original page either, since cascading a target you already own is a normal self-service action, not an admin one). Could not live-verify the second table (ownedTargets) with a real row end-to-end: creating a real temporary `targets` row requires a real `cycle_id` (NOT NULL since migration 20260730000003), and a direct query confirmed **zero real `evaluation_cycles` rows currently exist in production** -- inventing one just for this UI check was judged out of scope and too large a footprint for a small mirror-fix; verified instead via `npx tsc --noEmit`/`npx eslint` passing clean and a careful line-by-line diff confirming the JSX is an exact structural match to the already-verified ownedSubGoals fix and the original page's own long-standing, already-shipped markup. The ownedSubGoals fix itself WAS verified live end-to-end with the real temporary account and real production data, confirming the restored link renders and points at the correct real sub-goal id. All temporary test data (1 auth user, 1 profile, 1 user_roles row, 1 org_structure_assignments row) deleted afterward, confirmed zero residual rows; the real production position/sub-goal/KPI were reconfirmed unaffected throughout (only a read-side rendering fix, no data was written to any of them). `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (142 tests, unchanged -- pure JSX restoration, no logic change) all passed clean.

- **Fixed the "Add KPI" form on `/kpis/manage-kpis` not clearing after a successful submission** (2026-08-01), per a direct screenshot report: "إذا أضفت بيانات المؤشر احذف البيانات في النموذج ليكون جاهزا لاستقبال بيانات جديدة" -- the just-submitted title/unit/target/weight stayed sitting in the uncontrolled inputs after a real success, making it look like the KPI hadn't actually been added and risking an accidental duplicate resubmit. Root cause was the same established pattern already documented elsewhere in this codebase (EmployeeInviteForm.tsx's own comment): `ManageKpisPanel.tsx`'s add-KPI form deliberately intercepts `onSubmit` with `event.preventDefault()` + a manual `formAction(formData)` call (needed so a validation ERROR doesn't wipe the form, unlike React 19's native `<form action={fn}>` behavior) -- but that same interception means nothing ever clears the fields on SUCCESS either, since the browser's own native reset-on-submit never fires. Fixed with the exact `formRef` + `useEffect(() => { if (state?.status === "success") formRef.current?.reset(); }, [state])` idiom already used by `AssignGoalForm`/`BauTaskForm`/`CreateRecommendationForm`/`Feedback360Form`/`EmployeeInviteForm`/`ImportOrgStructureExcelForm` -- this component was simply the one that had never picked up that pattern. The request's "افعل ذلك في كل النماذج التي على هذه الصورة" (do this for every form in this image) was checked literally: the page's only other input surface, `AnnualTargetRow`'s inline target/actual save, is a CONTROLLED form (`useState`) that intentionally keeps showing the saved value after success (matching every other inline-edit row in this app, e.g. `EditRoleForm`) rather than an add-and-repeat form, so clearing it would be wrong, not missing -- confirmed this is the only literal `<form>` in the component before treating the fix as complete. Verified live end-to-end against the real shared database with a real temporary `strategy_admin` test account and the real sub-goal "تفعيل منظومة مدفوعة بالأداء": submitted a real temporary KPI through the actual form, confirmed the success message ("تمت إضافة المؤشر بنجاح.") appeared AND all four text/number fields read back empty via direct DOM inspection immediately after, then confirmed the row genuinely existed in the database (not just a client-side illusion) before deleting it as a throwaway test row. All temporary test data (1 auth user, 1 profile, 1 user_roles row, 1 strategic_kpis row) removed afterward, confirmed zero residual rows; the real pre-existing KPI on that sub-goal was reconfirmed untouched throughout. `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (142 tests, unchanged -- no new pure function needed dedicated coverage beyond the live-verified reset behavior, matching how the identical pattern was verified for every other form that already uses it) all passed clean.

- **Two UX fixes on the Career Path job-title detail screen, from two direct requests in the same round** (2026-08-02). (1) "ضع الجدارات حسب التصنيف" (put the competencies according to classification): `JobTitleCompetenciesManager`'s assigned-competencies list rendered flat, with no pillar grouping at all, even though the ADD dropdown right below it already grouped its own options by pillar via optgroups (`byPillar`). Fixed by extending the assigned-competencies query in `career-path/job-titles/[id]/page.tsx` to also carry `domain_id` (previously only `name_ar`) and resolving each row's pillar through the same `domainPillar` lookup the page already built for the picker, then grouping the assigned list by that pillar in the component (alphabetically sorted, since `competency_pillars` has no display_order column to order by). (2) "اضف خاصية البحث بحيث اضع الحروف الاولى فيعطيني الوظائف المطابقة للبحث": the same page's "روابط المسار الوظيفي" (career path links) section has a target-job-title `<select>` listing all ~358 company-wide job titles with no way to filter it, making it impractical to find one specific title by scrolling (a live screenshot showed the open dropdown scrolled to a wall of grade-16 titles). Added a text search input above `CareerPathEdgesManager`'s target select that narrows the SAME select's own option list by substring match against `nameAr` (not startsWith -- same "substring, not startsWith" convention as the Employees list's own 2026-07-24 search), rather than replacing it with a custom combobox component; the currently-selected option is re-derived on every render (`effectiveTargetId`) so a selection that no longer matches the search text falls back to the first remaining match instead of silently pointing at a hidden option, and a genuinely empty match set disables the select with a "لا توجد نتائج مطابقة" placeholder instead of rendering an empty, technically-selectable option. Verified live end-to-end against the real shared database with a real temporary `super_admin` test account and the real "رئيس تنفيذي للقطاع" job title (which genuinely has 6 assigned competencies across 2 real pillars): the assigned list rendered as two correctly-grouped sections ("ابتكار" with 1 item, "دعم" with 5 items) instead of one flat list of 6; the target search input, confirmed present in the live DOM with the correct placeholder text, narrowed the real 358-option select down to exactly 6 matches on "مدير عام", correctly showed the "لا توجد نتائج مطابقة" disabled state for a deliberately non-matching query, and correctly restored all 358 options when the search was cleared. `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (142 tests, unchanged -- pure JSX/derived-state changes with no new pure function needing dedicated coverage beyond what was already live-verified) all passed clean. All temporary test data (1 auth user, 1 profile, 1 user_roles row) deleted afterward, confirmed zero residual rows; the real production job title's competencies and career-path edges were reconfirmed unaffected throughout (both fixes are read/rendering-side only, no data was written to either table during verification).

- **Four fixes on the Career Path job-title create/edit screens from one round of feedback with four screenshots** (2026-08-03). (1) "ضبط لي النموذج ليكون مثل نموذج اضافة موظف": `JobTitleCoreForm`, `JobTitleDescriptionForm`, `JobTitleCompetenciesManager`, `CareerPathEdgesManager`, `CreateJobTitleForm`, and `StagedCompetenciesPicker` all used this screen's own one-off plain `block text-sm font-medium mb-1` labels and Tailwind-utility-class inputs instead of the `sru-formsection`/`sru-formgrid`/`sru-field` pattern EmployeeInviteForm and every other multi-field form in this app already use — restyled all six to match, including moving each form's own heading (previously duplicated by the host page's separate `<h2>`+`sru-card` wrapper) into its own `sru-formsection-head` with an icon badge, and removing the now-redundant wrappers from `career-path/job-titles/[id]/page.tsx`. (2) "مساعد الذكاء الاصطناعي لا يعمل": found `suggestJobDescription` (`career-path/job-titles/actions.ts`) was calling `model: "claude-opus-4-8"` — not a real model id — fixed to `claude-opus-5`. Investigating further (not something fixable from this session) revealed this was NOT the actual live failure: the real server log showed `Error: Could not resolve authentication method... Expected one of apiKey...`, meaning **`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is not set at all** in this project's environment (`grep` of the shared `.env.local` confirms zero matches, and `new Anthropic()` constructs successfully but with `apiKey: false`) — every single AI-suggest call has been failing on missing credentials since this feature was first built, with the wrong-model-id bug layered on top and never actually reached. Flagged directly to the project owner as something requiring a real Anthropic API key to be added to the server's `.env.local` (no rebuild needed, unlike `NEXT_PUBLIC_*` vars, since this is read server-side at request time — just needs the process to see the new env var, e.g. via `pm2 restart` with an updated env file) — not something fixable from this session. (3) "الجدارات الأساسية تظهر بشكل تلقائي والمطلوب مني تحديد المستوى وإضافة الجدارات الاخرى مثل التخصصية": the existing job-title EDIT screen required manually finding and adding all ~11 `type='core'` (institutional) competencies one at a time through the same dropdown used for specialized ones — real production data confirmed several job titles had only some of the 11 core competencies assigned, since nothing surfaced the gap. Extended `[id]/page.tsx`'s query to also select `competencies.type` and compute `coreCompetencies` (mirroring `new/page.tsx`'s already-existing pattern for the CREATE flow, which already pre-stages core competencies via `StagedCompetenciesPicker` — this gap was specific to the EDIT screen). `JobTitleCompetenciesManager` now always lists all 11 core competencies regardless of assignment status: already-assigned ones show their level + a delete icon as before; not-yet-assigned ones show an inline level `<select>` (new `CoreCompetencyRow` component) that calls the existing `assignJobTitleCompetency` action the moment a level is chosen — no separate "add" click needed. The specialized-competencies list below it (pillar-grouped, from PR #123) now explicitly excludes core ones, and the manual "add" dropdown is scoped to specialized-only, since core competencies are always already listed above. (4) "اضف خاصية البحث بحيث اضع الحروف الاولى فيعطيني الوظائف المطابقة للبحث": `CareerPathEdgesManager`'s target-job-title select already got this search fix in PR #123, but `CreateJobTitleForm`'s own SEPARATE link-target select (used only when creating a brand new job title with an optional initial career-path link) was a different, never-touched instance of the identical ~358-option unfiltered `<select>` -- added the same substring-search-narrows-the-select technique there too.

- **Found and fixed a real bug live during verification, not assumed**: `CoreCompetencyRow`'s level-placeholder option used `t("selectLevelPlaceholder")` against the `CareerPathJobTitleDetailPage` namespace, but that key only existed under the unrelated `CareerPathNewJobTitlePage` namespace — confirmed live via the real rendered text showing the raw string `CareerPathJobTitleDetailPage.selectLevelPlaceholder` instead of "اختر المستوى..." (and the exact `MISSING_MESSAGE` error in the dev server log) — added the missing key to the correct namespace in both locales, reverified the real placeholder text renders correctly afterward. Verified live end-to-end against the real shared database with a real temporary `super_admin` test account and the real "رئيس تنفيذي للقطاع" job title (confirmed beforehand via direct query to have exactly 6 of 11 core competencies assigned): the restyled sections rendered correctly, the الجدارات المؤسسية list showed all 11 with the 6 missing ones showing the (now-fixed) level placeholder and the 5 assigned ones showing their level + delete icon, selecting a level for a missing one (خدمة العميل) immediately created a real `job_title_competencies` row (confirmed via direct query, `required_level: "practitioner"`) with no separate save action, and the specialized list below correctly showed only the one non-core assigned competency (`التخطيط والتطوير`) instead of the previous mixed list of 6. The create-job-title page's own link-target search narrowed a real 359-option select to 5 matches on "عميد". The AI-suggest button was confirmed to still fail gracefully with the same user-facing message post-fix (expected, since the missing API key is a separate, unfixed blocker) — the real server-side error changed from a model-not-found-shaped failure to the expected authentication error, confirming the model id fix is correct and the API key really is the remaining blocker. All temporary test data (1 auth user, 1 profile, 1 user_roles row, and the one test `job_title_competencies` row created during verification) removed afterward, confirmed zero residual rows; the real job title's original 6 assigned competencies were reconfirmed unchanged. `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (142 tests, unchanged -- pure JSX/query changes with no new pure function needing dedicated coverage beyond what was live-verified) all passed clean.

- **Checked the Promotions module end-to-end and fixed the same unsearchable-job-title-select gap already fixed twice this session in career-path components** (2026-08-03): direct request to check `/promotions` after the career-path job-title-detail work (PRs #123/#124) — `promotions/page.tsx` (list), `PromotionReviewActions.tsx` (approve/reject), and `promotions/history/page.tsx` all worked correctly with no changes needed, verified live with two real temporary test accounts (a `manager`-role proposer, a `ceo`-role approver) through the actual propose -> approve -> history flow, including creating and then deleting a real temporary `evaluation_cycles` row since production still has zero real cycles (a long-documented gap, unrelated to this check). `ProposePromotionForm.tsx`’s `fromJobTitleId`/`toJobTitleId` selects, however, listed all 336+ real job titles with zero filtering — the identical UX gap already fixed for `CareerPathEdgesManager`/`CreateJobTitleForm` earlier the same day. Applied the exact same established pattern (a text input narrows the `<select>`’s own option list by substring match on `name_ar`, with a derived-during-render fallback keeping the selection valid as the filtered set shrinks, and a disabled “لا توجد نتائج مطابقة” placeholder when nothing matches) to both selects — the only structural difference from the career-path version is that `fromJobTitleId` is optional (keeps its own “-- لا يوجد --” empty option unconditionally alongside the filtered results) while `toJobTitleId` stays `required` and disables entirely when the filter matches nothing. Two new shared keys (`jobTitleSearchPlaceholder`/`jobTitleNoMatches`) added under `ProposePromotionPage` in both locales rather than per-field keys, since both selects share the exact same job-title list and placeholder copy. Verified live end-to-end against the real shared database with a real temporary `manager` test account and a second real temporary evaluation cycle (created and deleted solely for this check): confirmed the search narrowed the real 360-option list to exactly 1 match (“مدير التحول الرقمي”) plus the placeholder, confirmed the no-match state correctly disabled the `toJobTitleId` select with the “no matches” option, confirmed clearing the search correctly restored all 360 options, and confirmed a full real submission through the filtered selection created the exact intended `promotions` row. `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (142 tests, unchanged — pure JSX/state addition mirroring an already-established, previously-tested pattern) all passed clean. All temporary test data (1 auth user, 1 profile, its `user_roles` row, 1 evaluation cycle, 1 promotion row) deleted afterward, confirmed zero residual rows in every touched table.

- **Career Path job-titles list: live as-you-type search, plus Excel import + empty-template download** (2026-08-03), from a direct request: "طور في عملية البحث بحيث مجرد ادخل الحروف تبدأ تظهر لي جميع الكلمات التي بدأت تطابق الكلمة" (make search results appear as soon as letters are typed) and "اضف لي زرين أحدهما للاستيراد من اكسل والاخر لتحميل نموذج فارغ" (add two buttons: one to import from Excel, one to download an empty template), both explicitly scoped to /career-path/job-titles.

- **Search**: the page's old <form method="get"> (a full page reload per query, via the q search param) is replaced by JobTitlesTable.tsx, a new client component receiving the already-fetched ~360 rows as props and filtering them in a useMemo on every keystroke (onChange, not onSubmit) -- same AdminUsersTable.tsx-established live-filter convention already used for the Users tab on /admin, minus pagination (not requested here, and this list has never paginated). Verified live that the filtered count narrows progressively as each character of a real query is typed (م:327 -> مد:46 -> مدير:44 -> مدير ع:6) and restores to the full 359 rows when cleared -- genuinely reactive, not a one-shot check.

- **Import**: two literal, separate header buttons (not one modal-trigger bundling a download link inside, unlike ImportOrgStructureExcelForm's established shape) -- "تحميل نموذج فارغ" is a plain <a href=... download> to a new public/templates/sru-job-titles-import-template.xlsx (generated via a one-off exceljs script, not committed -- same precedent as every other template file in this repo), and "استيراد من إكسل" opens a <dialog> modal (ImportJobTitlesExcelForm.tsx) hosting only the file-picker/submit step. The new importJobTitlesExcel Server Action (career-path/job-titles/import-actions.ts) covers exactly the fields CreateJobTitleForm/JobTitleCoreForm already expose -- name_ar/name_en/job family (matched by exact trimmed name against real job_families rows, not auto-created, same "best-effort now, manual correction later" precedent as the 2026-07-24 org_units matching)/grade (1-16)/category (accepts either the real Arabic UI labels -- قيادية/أكاديمية/إدارية/تقنية/عمالية -- or the raw enum key)/qualification/description -- deliberately NOT job_title_competencies or career_path edges, which stay per-job-title screens (this sheet shape has no clean way to express either without a source file to design against, mirroring the org-structure import's own staffing-is-embedded-but-nothing-else scoping choice). job_titles has a PLAIN UNIQUE(job_family_id, name_ar) (not a partial index like org_structure_positions.external_code/evaluation_scores/calibration_results), so the ON-CONFLICT limitation that forced those tables' select-then-insert-or-update workaround doesn't apply here -- but the same select-first approach was still used anyway, needed to (a) report accurate create-vs-update counts and (b) reset career_content_status to 'draft' on any update, mirroring updateJobTitleCore's own established behavior for a manual edit. Every write goes through the caller's own RLS-respecting client -- real authorization is job_titles_insert/update's existing careerPath>=prepare RLS, not this action's code; a row RLS rejects (or any other per-row error) becomes a row-level entry in the returned rowErrors summary rather than aborting the whole import, same bulk-error-collection discipline as importOrgStructureExcel.

- **A real, transient dev-environment race was hit and correctly diagnosed as such during verification, not misreported as a product bug**: the first two live import attempts both failed with a generic "invalid_input", the first with a genuine Bug: uncompressed data size mismatch JSZip error (traced to a corrupted manually-transcribed base64 test fixture -- a >8900-character string retyped by hand into a browser javascript_exec call, not a real upload-path defect) and the second failing with no such parse error at all despite temporary debug logging showing every column present and no missing-column branch firing -- strongly suggesting a Turbopack hot-reload race (the action module was still mid-swap from an edit moments earlier) rather than the shipped code. Switched the test fixture to a byte-exact same-origin fetch() of a temporary file copied into public/ (eliminating any manual-transcription risk entirely) and, once the dev server had fully settled after the last edit, the identical file imported cleanly on the first attempt -- confirmed twice more (a create, then a same-file-modified-in-place re-import correctly reporting "0 new, 1 updated" against the exact same row id, not a duplicate). Verified live end-to-end against the real shared database with a real temporary hr_admin test account: created a real temporary job title via the actual import flow (all 7 fields -- name_ar/name_en/family/grade/category/qualification/description -- confirmed correct via direct query), then re-imported a modified version of the identical file and confirmed the SAME row's qualification/description updated in place rather than a new row appearing. The generated template's headers were also round-tripped through exceljs's own reader before use, confirming all 4 required columns (اسم المسمى الوظيفي/العائلة الوظيفية/الدرجة/الفئة) are present and correctly named. All temporary test data (1 auth user, 1 profile, its user_roles row, and the one temporary job_titles row created/updated during verification) deleted afterward, confirmed zero residual rows in every touched table; production's real ~359 job titles were reconfirmed unaffected throughout (this feature only ever wrote the one clearly-named temporary test row). npx tsc --noEmit, npx eslint, and the full vitest run suite (142 tests, unchanged -- pure JSX/Server-Action addition with no new pure function needing dedicated unit coverage beyond what was live-verified) all passed clean.

- **Career Path page rebuilt as named, drillable tracks (replacing the flat career_path table), plus app-wide hamza-insensitive search** (2026-08-04), from live feedback on the management (prepare+) view of /career-path: "لا اريد الصفحة تظهر بهذا الشكل وانما اريدها تظهر اسم المسار وعند الضغط عليه يطلع له مسار زمني في جميع الوظائف الممكنة وعند الضغط على الوظيفة يظهر المتطلبات الخاصة بها" (don't show the flat from/to/requirements table -- show track names; clicking one shows a timeline of every possible job in it; clicking a job shows its own requirements) plus a separate live-search request. A "مسار" (track) is defined as one entry point into the career_path graph -- a job title that never appears on the receiving side of any edge -- computed by the new pure `findCareerPathTrackRoots` (src/lib/careerPathTracks.ts, unit-tested), which matches how the real data was actually built (each specialty's own ladder chained one grade at a time across many earlier migrations) without needing a schema column that doesn't exist. The timeline itself reuses `buildForwardCareerTree`, the exact graph-walk already built for the self-scoped employee career-path view -- no new graph logic duplicated. New client component `CareerPathTracksExplorer.tsx`: an ungated list of track cards (name + grade, live-searchable) that, on click, renders the forward timeline with per-job click-to-reveal requirements (collapsed by default, matching the literal ask).

- **A real, explicitly-reported layout bug was found and fixed before this shipped, not assumed correct**: a first version rendered every node-with-children as its own new nested branch, which -- for an ORDINARY non-branching chain (the common case, most nodes have exactly one next step) -- duplicated nearly every node once as a "child chip" and again as the head of its own redundant branch. Caught via the project owner's own live screenshot of a real fan-out case ("محلل أعمال مساعد" leading to both "محلل بيانات" and "محلل أعمال") explicitly asking that the SOURCE job render once, with its multiple destinations threaded beside each other in one row, not each destination repeating the source on its own row -- exactly the flat table's original defect, now reproduced by the naive first tree version. Fixed with a chain-flattening approach: `CareerPathTimelineBranch` walks forward through single-child links as one continuous horizontal row (no branching, no duplication) and only at a REAL fan-out point (a node with >1 children) does that same row also carry every child beside each other; only children that themselves branch further start a new indented row below (recursing this same logic from that child) -- a plain single-path continuation never repeats a node or starts a nested row. Verified live against the real production data (confirmed via direct query: exactly 2 real fan-out sources exist in career_path today, "قائد فريق خدمة العملاء" fanning to 3 destinations and "محلل أعمال مساعد" to 2) with a real temporary hr_admin test account: DOM bounding-rect inspection confirmed both real fan-out groups render with all their destination chips at the identical vertical position (one shared horizontal row), not stacked as separate rows, while the surrounding single-path segments (e.g. مدير مركز الاتصال -> ... after the 3-way fan-out) correctly continue as their own single rows with no duplicated chips.

- **Separately, a mid-session follow-up request** ("اريدك ان تهمل الهمزة سواء استخدمها المستخدم ام لا" -- ignore the hamza whether the user typed it or not) was applied as a new shared `src/lib/arabicSearch.ts` (`foldArabicHamza`/`includesIgnoringHamza`, unit-tested: folds أ/إ/آ to ا, ؤ to و, ئ to ي, removes a standalone ء, no broader normalization since only hamza was asked for) and retrofitted into every existing Arabic-name live-search filter in the app, not just the new tracks explorer: `JobTitlesTable.tsx`, `ProposePromotionForm.tsx` (both job-title selects), `CareerPathEdgesManager.tsx`, `CreateJobTitleForm.tsx`, the Employees list's server-side name filter, and `AdminUsersTable.tsx` -- English name fields and numeric employee numbers were deliberately left untouched (hamza folding is meaningless there). Verified live: searching "ادارة بيانات" (no hamza) on the new tracks list correctly matched the real "أ.إدارة بيانات" track (hamza on the alef). All temporary test data (1 auth user, 1 profile, its user_roles row) deleted afterward, confirmed zero residual rows; production's real 157 career_path edges were reconfirmed unchanged throughout (this was a pure read-side rendering rebuild, no data written). `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (154 tests -- 12 new: 6 for findCareerPathTrackRoots, 6 for arabicSearch) all passed clean.

- **Career Path timeline rebuilt as a click-to-expand accordion, plus Excel import for career_path edges** (2026-08-04), from direct live feedback on the just-shipped tracks explorer (PR #127): "محلل أعمال مساعد ذكرت مرتين لانها تتجه لوظيفتين، اجعلها في كرت واحد وعند الضغط عليها تظهر التفاصيل وفيها الوظيفتين اللتين يستطيع الترقية عليها ... اريد الوظيفتين محلل اعمال ومحلل بيانات تظهران فوق بعضهم" (the fan-out source read as duplicated; make it one card, click it to reveal its destination options, stacked ABOVE each other -- فوق بعضهم -- not beside each other). This directly supersedes PR #127's own always-fully-expanded horizontal-thread design ("بجانب بعضهم"), which the project owner had asked for in an earlier round but reported back as still confusing once real multi-level fan-out was visible all at once. `CareerPathTimelineBranch`/`TimelineNodeChip`'s chain-flattening logic was replaced entirely by a single recursive `TimelineAccordionNode`: every node (the root included) starts collapsed as one card; clicking it reveals its own requirements (or a "start of track" note for the root) and, if it has any, its next-step options -- each rendered as its own collapsed card stacked directly below, one per row, itself independently expandable the same way. A non-branching chain naturally reveals one card at a time as the user drills down ("يتضح المسار العام بعد تفريعه حتى يصل الدرجات العليا" -- the overall path becomes clear as it's expanded, up to the top grades); a real fan-out reveals all its options together, stacked, the first time it's expanded -- no special-casing needed for either case, and nothing is ever rendered twice.

- **Import**: two literal header buttons ("استيراد من إكسل"/"تحميل نموذج فارغ"), matching the exact shape already established for the job-titles page's own import feature, added to `/career-path` itself so the project owner can bulk-load real career_path data ("لكي ازودك بالبيانات المطلوبة") rather than only entering edges one at a time via `CareerPathEdgesManager`. New `importCareerPathExcel` Server Action (`career-path/import-actions.ts`): one row per edge (from/to job title name + optional requirements), job titles matched by EXACT name against real `job_titles` rows -- flagged and skipped (not guessed) if a name matches more than one row, since `job_titles.name_ar` is only unique per job family. `career_path` has a PLAIN `UNIQUE(from_job_title_id, to_job_title_id)` (not partial), so this doesn't strictly need the select-then-insert-or-update workaround purely to dodge an ON CONFLICT limitation -- but selecting existing edges first is still used, to report accurate create-vs-update counts and to update `requirements_ar` in place on a re-import rather than erroring on the unique constraint. Every write goes through the caller's own RLS-respecting client (`careerPath>=prepare`, same bar as `job_titles`); crucially, the import buttons sit in the page's always-visible header, NOT gated behind `roots.length > 0`, since the whole point is bootstrapping data when the track list is still empty.

- **A real mistake was made and self-caught during verification, not silently absorbed**: the first live import test used a real, already-existing job-title pair ("عامل"->"مساند") without checking beforehand whether an edge already existed between them -- it did, and the test import genuinely overwrote its real `requirements_ar` in production with test text before this was noticed. Root-caused immediately by grepping the original data-population migration (`20260720000002_career_path_data.sql`) for that exact edge, confirming its originally-seeded value was `NULL`, and restoring it via a direct, verified UPDATE -- confirmed byte-for-byte back to the original state afterward. The remaining verification (the create path, using a real temporary edge between two job titles confirmed via a direct query to have NO pre-existing edge before the test) was completed correctly, and the accordion behavior was verified via real DOM bounding-rect inspection against the actual `محلل أعمال مساعد` fan-out: all three cards in the two-way fan-out (root + its 2 destinations) render at identical horizontal position with increasing vertical position -- genuinely stacked, not side-by-side -- and drilling three levels deep correctly revealed the real 3-way fan-out (`قائد فريق خدمة العملاء` -> 3 destinations) the same way. The import feature itself was verified end-to-end for both paths: a real create ("1 new, 0 updated", confirmed via direct query, then deleted) and, incidentally, a real update (via the same-pair mistake above, since re-corrected). All temporary test data (1 auth user, 1 profile, its user_roles row, 1 genuinely-new test edge) deleted afterward, confirmed zero residual rows in the tables that should have none; the one real pre-existing edge was confirmed restored to its exact original state. `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (154 tests, unchanged -- this is a pure component-rewrite plus a new Server Action with no new pure function needing dedicated unit coverage beyond what was live-verified) all passed clean.

- **Salary Scale: fixed a real hidden-data bug (step_h/step_i never shown), added live hamza-insensitive search** (2026-08-04), from a direct "check the salary scale page too" request. Found while reading the page, not reported by the project owner: the 20260720000001 data migration added nullable `step_h`/`step_i` columns to `salary_scale` for academic titles' real 9-step scale (A-I -- admin tracks stay at 7 steps, A-G) and populated real values for 9 real rows (confirmed live: `أستاذ` has step_h=51673/step_i=55648, `أستاذ مشارك` and `أستاذ مساعد` similarly), but `/salary-scale`'s own query only ever selected `step_a`..`step_g` and the table only ever rendered columns A-G -- silently hiding two real, populated salary columns for every academic professor-tier title since that migration shipped, with no code change since. Fixed by extending the SELECT and adding H/I columns to a new `SalaryScaleTable` client component (rendering "—" for the ~327 non-academic rows where they're genuinely NULL); the subtitle text (previously claiming "(أ–ز)", i.e. A-G only) was corrected to "(أ–ط)" with an explanatory sentence distinguishing the two real track lengths.

- **Also added live, hamza-insensitive search** (`SalaryScaleTable`, reusing `arabicSearch.ts`) -- this ~336-row table had no search at all before, the same gap already fixed on the job-titles and career-path pages this session. Verified live end-to-end against the real shared database with a real temporary hr_admin test account: confirmed the real `أستاذ`/`أستاذ مشارك`/`أستاذ مساعد` rows now show their real step_h/step_i values instead of being silently truncated at G, confirmed every other row correctly shows "—" for both new columns, and confirmed the search narrows correctly (a query for "أستاذ" and, separately, the hamza-free "استاذ" both correctly returned the same 3 real matching rows). All temporary test data (1 auth user, 1 profile, its user_roles row) deleted afterward, confirmed zero residual rows; no table data was written or modified during this check (a pure read-side fix). `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (154 tests, unchanged -- pure JSX/query changes reusing the already-tested `includesIgnoringHamza` utility, no new pure function needing dedicated coverage) all passed clean.

- **Separately, a live follow-up question was asked and answered directly, no code change requested**: how to represent one job title fanning out to multiple next steps (the same "محلل أعمال مساعد" example from the accordion redesign) in the career-path Excel import template — answered that the existing flat one-row-per-edge template already supports this natively: two separate rows sharing the same "من" value with different "إلى"/"المتطلبات" produce two independent edges from the same source, exactly reconstructing a fan-out, with no template or code change needed.

- **Extended the job-titles Excel import to also set institutional competency levels** (2026-08-04), from direct feedback on the just-shipped import feature (PR #126): "لم يتطرق الى الجدارات" (it didn't address the competencies), pointing at the job-title detail page's own "الجدارات المؤسسية" (institutional competencies) section. Added 11 new OPTIONAL columns to the import, one per real `type='core'` competency (fetched live from the database at template-generation time, not hardcoded), named after the competency's own name_ar, each holding a required_level label (أساسي/ممارس/متقدم/محترف). Deliberately scoped to core competencies only, not specialized ones -- the same 11 apply to every job title (mirroring `JobTitleCompetenciesManager`'s own core-vs-specialized split), so a fixed column set works; specialized competencies vary per pillar/domain and would need a fundamentally different sheet shape (one row per job-title+competency pair) that wasn't asked for here. A blank competency cell is skipped entirely -- an existing assignment is left untouched, never cleared, matching org_structure_assignments' established "add/update only" import discipline. Implementation: `job_titles` are re-fetched by (job_family_id, name_ar) AFTER the insert/update pass completes, since a batch INSERT's returned row order isn't safe to match back against the original parsed rows one-to-one; `job_title_competencies` DOES have a partial unique index (`(job_title_id, competency_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL`, unlike job_titles' own plain unique constraint), so its assignment pass uses the same select-then-insert-or-update workaround already established for evaluation_scores/calibration_results. `JobTitlesImportResult`'s summary gained a `competenciesSet` count, surfaced in the success message and the template's own instructions sheet (which also explains, per a separate same-day question, that a job title fanning out to multiple next steps still belongs in the separate career_path import, not this one). Verified live end-to-end against the real shared database with a real temporary hr_admin test account: imported a real temporary job title with two core competency columns filled ("التواصل الفعال"=متقدم, "روح الفريق"=أساسي) -- confirmed via direct query both `job_title_competencies` rows were created with the correct levels; re-imported a modified version of the same file changing one level to محترف -- confirmed via direct query the SAME row (same id) was updated in place (advanced -> professional), not duplicated, while the untouched competency stayed unchanged. (One test-methodology false alarm along the way, not a product bug: the browser's rendered success text after the second import briefly showed a stale pre-update message due to a page-text read racing the actual server response -- the direct database check immediately after proved the real write had already completed correctly.) All temporary test data (1 auth user, 1 profile, its user_roles row, 1 job_titles row plus its 2 job_title_competencies children -- deleted in FK-safe order, child rows first, since job_title_competencies.job_title_id is ON DELETE RESTRICT) removed afterward, confirmed zero residual rows. `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (154 tests, unchanged -- pure Server Action extension with no new pure function needing dedicated unit coverage beyond what was live-verified) all passed clean.

- **Vacancies create-form job-title select gained the same search fix already applied to career-path/promotions forms** (2026-08-04): direct request to check `/vacancies` after the earlier round of unsearchable-select fixes. `/vacancies/page.tsx` (list) and `/vacancies/new/actions.ts` (`createVacancy`) both checked out clean with no changes needed, verified by reading them in full. `CreateVacancyForm.tsx`'s `jobTitleId` `<select>`, however, listed all 336+ real job titles with zero filtering -- the identical gap already fixed in `JobTitlesTable.tsx`/`ProposePromotionForm.tsx`/`CareerPathEdgesManager.tsx`/`CreateJobTitleForm.tsx`. Applied the same established pattern: a text input narrows the `<select>`'s own option list via `includesIgnoringHamza`, with a derived-during-render `effectiveJobTitleId` fallback keeping the (now-controlled) selection valid as the filtered set shrinks, and the select disables with a "لا توجد نتائج مطابقة" placeholder when nothing matches -- the `orgUnitId` select (58 org units) was left untouched, unsearchable-select fatigue only applies past a few hundred options, not 58. Two new keys added under `CreateVacancyPage` in both locales (`jobTitleSearchPlaceholder`/`jobTitleNoMatches`). Verified live end-to-end against the real shared database with a real temporary `hr_admin` test account (`vacancies_insert` requires `vacancies>=approve`, hr_admin-only per the seeded matrix): the search correctly narrowed the real 336-option list to exactly 6 matches on "مدير عام", confirmed hamza-insensitivity by typing "استشاري ادارة" (no hamza) and getting the two real hamza-bearing matches ("استشاري إدارة مشاريع"/"استشاري إدارة بيانات"), then selected "استشاري إدارة بيانات" from the filtered list, chose a real org unit, and submitted -- confirmed the real vacancy row was created with the correct `job_title_id`/`org_unit_id` and redirected correctly to the list page showing it. All temporary test data (1 auth user, 1 profile, its `user_roles` row, 1 test vacancy row) deleted afterward, confirmed zero residual rows in every touched table. `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (154 tests, unchanged -- pure JSX/state addition mirroring an already-established, previously-tested pattern) all passed clean.

- **Fixed a real bug on `/recommendations`: creating a development/separation recommendation didn't update the page until a manual reload** (2026-08-04): direct request to check `/recommendations` after the vacancies round. Read the page, form, actions, and review-action component in full -- the VPRA/RLS design (self-row bypass, `check_vpra('promotions', level, ...)` mirroring `promotions`/`rewards` exactly), the create action, and the approve/reject action all checked out correct on paper. Live verification with a real temporary `manager` test account (holds `promotions=recommend` per the seeded matrix) caught a real bug static reading missed: submitting `CreateRecommendationForm` showed the real "تم إرسال التوصية بنجاح" success message, and the row WAS genuinely created in the database (confirmed via direct query), but the stat-card counts and the list table directly below stayed at their stale pre-submission values (0 / "لا توجد توصيات بعد") until the page was manually reloaded -- a real, user-visible contradiction between the success message and the visible page state. Root cause: `CreateRecommendationForm.tsx`'s success `useEffect` only called `formRef.current?.reset()`, never `router.refresh()` -- unlike `RecommendationReviewActions.tsx` (the approve/reject component on the same page), which already calls `router.refresh()` correctly and was confirmed working live. Fixed by adding `useRouter` from `@/i18n/navigation` and calling `router.refresh()` alongside the existing form reset in that same effect, matching the established pattern already used by `ImportJobTitlesExcelForm`/`ImportOrgStructureExcelForm` for this exact `useActionState`-based shape. Verified the fix live end-to-end: submitted a fresh recommendation through the actual form and confirmed the stat card count and the list row both appeared immediately with no manual reload, then confirmed the approve action (already correct) still updates the row's status/`approved_by` and re-renders correctly.

- **Incidental, unrelated finding, not investigated or touched**: the employee `<select>` on this page listed a profile named "TMPVAC1 -- حساب اختبار الشواغر" that doesn't match any test account created or cleaned up during this session's own work -- flagged as likely leftover test data from a different, concurrent session (this repo has multiple parallel Claude sessions working on it), not cleaned up here since deleting another session's in-progress test data without confirmation would risk destroying real in-flight verification. All of this session's own temporary test data (2 auth users/profiles/user_roles rows across two setup rounds, 2 test recommendation rows) deleted afterward, confirmed zero residual rows in every touched table. `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (154 tests, unchanged -- a client-side effect fix with no new pure function needing dedicated coverage beyond what was live-verified) all passed clean.

- **Fixed a real bug on `/calibration/new`: the create-session form was completely unreachable in production, and the shown reason was wrong** (2026-08-04): direct request to check `/calibration` after the recommendations round. `/calibration` (list) and `/calibration/[id]` (enter ratings, `saveCalibrationResults`) both read correctly on paper and were confirmed correct live -- the enter-ratings screen prefills from `defaultValue`-based inputs (same established design as `saveEvaluationScores`, no `router.refresh()` needed since the just-typed values remain in the uncontrolled inputs after the `useActionState` re-render). The real bug was on `/calibration/new`: its gate was `cycles && cycles.length > 0 && orgUnits && orgUnits.length > 0 ? <form> : <p>{t("errorForbidden")}</p>` -- collapsing two structurally different conditions (real lack of permission vs. simply no reference data to pick from yet) onto the identical "ليست لديك صلاحية إنشاء جلسات معايرة" (you don't have permission) message. Confirmed live this isn't a theoretical edge case: a direct query showed `evaluation_cycles` genuinely has **zero rows** in production today, meaning the create form's own `cycles.length > 0` condition can never pass -- **no one, including a real account holding `calibration=approve`, could ever see the create form**, and the message told them the wrong reason why.

- **A second, separate finding during verification, not assumed from memory**: this session's first test used an `hr_admin` test account, expecting to reproduce the bug with a real approve-level user per an earlier (2026-07-19) CLAUDE.md log entry -- but a live `role_permissions` query showed `hr_admin`'s *current* seeded level on `calibration` is `'recommend'`, not `'approve'` (a role_permissions matrix change that happened at some point after that original entry, not documented elsewhere in this file, and the query also surfaced a role code, `admin_off`, that doesn't appear anywhere in CLAUDE.md's own role table -- a custom role created via the already-built `/admin` role editor per CLAUDE.md §4-B, not investigated further since it wasn't relevant to this bug). `super_admin`/`ceo` are the two roles that currently hold `'approve'`, confirmed and used for the corrected test. This is a live illustration of why memory/documentation of `role_permissions` snapshots decays -- the matrix is genuinely DB-configurable and re-verifying against the real table beats trusting an old log entry. Fixed with a new, honest `errorNoData` message ("لا توجد دورات تقييم أو وحدات تنظيمية متاحة حاليًا -- يجب إنشاء دورة تقييم أولًا قبل إنشاء جلسة معايرة") shown only in the empty-reference-data branch, while the earlier, separate `!canCreate` check (unchanged) still correctly returns the real `errorForbidden` message for an account that genuinely lacks the `approve` grant. Verified live end-to-end against the real database: a real temporary `hr_admin` test account (confirmed `calibration=recommend`, correctly insufficient) saw the real, accurate `errorForbidden` message from the unchanged permission gate; a real temporary `super_admin` test account (confirmed `calibration=approve`) saw the NEW `errorNoData` message instead of the old misleading one; then seeded one real temporary `evaluation_cycles` row (`cycle_type='calendar'`, a column added by a same-day-unrelated migration, `20260728000001`, not previously known to this test script) and confirmed the exact same `super_admin` account now saw the full create form, successfully created a real session, and successfully entered and saved a real calibrated rating for the one real employee in that session's org unit (`calibration_results` row confirmed via direct query: `original_rating=80`, `calibrated_rating=85`, correct `justification`) -- proving the full create -> enter-ratings flow still works end-to-end, not just the error-message fix in isolation. All temporary test data (2 auth users/profiles/user_roles rows across two test rounds, 1 evaluation_cycles row, 1 calibration_sessions row, 1 calibration_results row -- the latter confirmed cascade-deleted via `calibration_results.session_id`'s own `ON DELETE CASCADE`, not manually) deleted afterward, confirmed zero residual rows in every touched table, real production `profiles` reconfirmed back to exactly 8. `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (154 tests, unchanged -- a page-level conditional-message fix with no new pure function needing dedicated coverage beyond what was live-verified) all passed clean.

- **Vacancy create form: restyled to the add-employee pattern, live job-title search, and requirements prefilled from the job title's own record** (2026-08-04), from three asks in one round with screenshots of /vacancies/new. (1) "حسن لي النموذج ليكون مثل اضافة موظف": `CreateVacancyForm` used its own one-off Tailwind-utility inputs and a flat field stack -- restyled to the same `sru-formsection`/`sru-formgrid`/`sru-field`/`sru-form-submitrow` pattern `EmployeeInviteForm` and every other multi-field form in this app already use, split into two labeled sections (بيانات الشاغر / المتطلبات) with icon badges; the page's own heading/subtitle were left untouched. (2) "مكن ظهور المسمى بمجرد كتابة الحروف الاولى وليس الحرف الاول فقط": the ~359-option job-title `<select>` had no filtering, so the browser's native first-letter jump was the only way to find a title -- added the same search-narrows-the-select pattern already established for `CareerPathEdgesManager`/`ProposePromotionForm`/`CreateJobTitleForm`/`JobTitlesTable`, reusing `includesIgnoringHamza` (substring, hamza-insensitive). Unlike those, this select keeps its "اختر المسمى الوظيفي" placeholder and does NOT auto-fall-back to the first filtered match -- a vacancy's job title is a deliberate choice, not a defaultable one -- and a selection the current search no longer matches is dropped rather than left hidden-but-submitted; an empty match set disables the select with a "لا توجد نتائج مطابقة" option. (3) "خذ متطلبات الوظيفة من أصل مكان الوظيفة": the requirements textarea was blank every time, so the admin retyped qualification text that `job_titles.qualification_required` already holds -- confirmed live against the real database that 265 of the 359 real job titles carry that text, and that its shape (e.g. "بكالوريوس خبرة 3 سنوات") matches exactly what the project owner was typing by hand in their own screenshot. The page now selects that column and the form prefills the textarea from the selected title, still fully editable.

- **`[استنتاج]` on the source**: `job_titles.qualification_required` (the title's own record, edited on `/career-path/job-titles/[id]`) was chosen over `career_path.requirements_ar` (per-edge "متطلبات الانتقال", which describes moving between two specific titles and would need an arbitrary pick among incoming edges) -- flagged to the project owner rather than assumed settled. Prefill deliberately never clobbers text the admin typed themselves: state is adjusted during render (React's documented derive-from-changed-value pattern, not an effect, which this repo's `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` rule rejects) and only overwrites an empty box or the exact text this form auto-filled for the previous title; a manually-edited box instead shows a "استعادة متطلبات المسمى الوظيفي" button, and a title with no recorded requirements shows an explicit note instead of silently doing nothing. Verified live end-to-end against the real shared database with a real temporary `hr_admin` test account (`vacancies=approve` is hr_admin-only per the seeded matrix): the search narrowed 359 options to 9 on "محلل" and to 1 on "محلل بيانات", the no-match state correctly disabled the select, selecting "محلل بيانات" prefilled the real "بكالوريوس خبرة 3 سنوات" with the source hint, a manual edit survived switching to a different job title (with the restore button appearing), clicking restore pulled in the newly-selected title's own real requirements, and a full real submission created the exact intended `vacancies` row (correct job title/org unit/requirements, `status='open'`) with the redirect landing on the list showing it. Could not capture a screenshot this round -- the Browser pane wasn't displayed, so it wasn't compositing frames (an environment limitation, reported rather than fabricated); everything above was verified via real DOM/state inspection and direct database queries instead. All test data (1 auth user, 1 profile, its `user_roles` row, and the one test vacancy) deleted afterward scoped to the exact created ids, confirmed zero residual rows (`vacancies` back to 0). `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (154 tests, unchanged -- pure JSX/derived-state changes reusing the already-tested `includesIgnoringHamza` helper) all passed clean.

- **Fixed the same class of silently-broken create form on `/promotions/new`, found while checking the promotions module** (2026-08-04): `/promotions` (list), `PromotionReviewActions.tsx`/`reviewPromotion` (approve/reject), and `/promotions/history` all read correctly and were confirmed correct live -- `ProposePromotionForm.tsx`'s job-title search fix from the same-day earlier PR (#125) was reconfirmed working. The real find: `ProposePromotionPage`'s render gate only checked `employees.length > 0 && jobTitles.length > 0`, never `cycles.length > 0`, even though `cycleId` is `required` on both the `<select>` and the Zod schema (`proposePromotionSchema`, no `.optional()`). With `evaluation_cycles` genuinely at zero rows in production (the same root cause already fixed for `/calibration/new` earlier the same day), the form rendered anyway with a cycle dropdown holding only its own disabled placeholder option -- confirmed live via `cycleSelect.checkValidity()`/`.validationMessage` returning `"Please select an item in the list."`, a raw, unlocalized native English browser validation bubble on an otherwise fully Arabic RTL form, for a real `hr_admin` test account that genuinely holds `promotions=recommend` and full `employeeData` visibility. Fixed the same way as calibration: added `cycles.length > 0` to the success condition, with a distinct honest `errorNoData` message ("لا توجد دورات تقييم متاحة حاليًا -- يجب إنشاء دورة تقييم أولًا قبل اقتراح ترقية") shown specifically when cycles are the empty one, leaving the real `!canPropose` branch's `errorForbidden` message untouched.

- **A separate, real permission-driven gap was found and deliberately NOT fixed, flagged instead**: a live `role_permissions` query for `cxo` (one of the three non-approve roles -- `cxo`/`hr_admin`/`manager` -- that can reach `canPropose`) showed it holds `promotions=recommend` and `careerPath=recommend` (explaining why `job_titles` render fine for it) but **no `employeeData` grant at all** -- meaning `profiles_select`'s RLS only exposes a `cxo` user their own self-row, so the "employee to promote" dropdown silently contains exactly one option (themselves), with no error or explanation, structurally preventing a `cxo` from ever proposing a promotion for anyone but themselves. Confirmed live with a real temporary `cxo` test account (the employee select genuinely showed only their own name). This is a `role_permissions` matrix/business-permission question, not a code defect the way the cycles issue was -- matching this project's established discipline of flagging rather than unilaterally changing the seeded matrix (the same posture taken for `goals`/`bau_tasks`' deliberately under-granted supervisor/manager RLS) -- not changed here, flagged for the project owner to decide whether `cxo` should hold some `employeeData` level. Verified live end-to-end against the real shared database: a real temporary `cxo` test account correctly hit the narrow self-row employee list (unrelated to the fix, a separate finding); a real temporary `hr_admin` test account (full `employeeData=approve`) saw the honest `errorNoData` message with zero real cycles, then -- after seeding one real temporary `evaluation_cycles` row -- saw the full form render, successfully created a real promotion (`محلل بيانات`, grade 9, for a real employee), got redirected to `/promotions` showing the new pending row, successfully approved it (`status`/`approved_by` confirmed correct), and confirmed the decision appears correctly on `/promotions/history`. All temporary test data (2 auth users/profiles/user_roles rows across the cxo and hr_admin rounds, 1 evaluation_cycles row, 1 promotions row) deleted afterward, confirmed zero residual rows in every touched table.

- **Incidental cross-session observation, not caused by this session's own work**: the "TMPVAC1" leftover test profile flagged as a concurrent session's artifact in the immediately preceding recommendations-check entry was found to have disappeared during this session's own verification -- confirmed none of this session's delete calls ever referenced that profile's id, and a fresh `git fetch` showed a concurrent PR (#134, "Polish vacancy form") had landed and merged in the interim, consistent with that other session having cleaned up its own test data independently; the real 7 production employees were reconfirmed unchanged throughout. `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (154 tests, unchanged -- a page-level conditional-message fix mirroring the already-tested calibration pattern, with no new pure function needing dedicated coverage) all passed clean.

- **Excel import + empty template for `vacancies`** (2026-08-04): the /vacancies page had create-one-at-a-time entry only, unlike `job_titles`/`career_path`/employees/org-structure which all gained bulk import earlier. Added `src/app/[locale]/(app)/vacancies/import-actions.ts` (`importVacanciesExcel`), `src/components/ImportVacanciesExcelForm.tsx`, two header buttons on `/vacancies` (a plain download link + a `<dialog>` import modal, the exact shape already established for career-path/job-titles), and `public/templates/sru-vacancies-import-template.xlsx` (generated by a one-off `exceljs` script, not committed, same precedent as every other template in this repo -- round-tripped back through the action's own header-matching logic before shipping). Columns: المسمى الوظيفي + الوحدة التنظيمية (required, exact-name matched against the real tables) plus optional العائلة الوظيفية / الحالة / المتطلبات.

- **The job-family column is a real addition over the career-path import's shape, driven by checking the real data first**: 4 of the 359 real `job_titles` genuinely share a `name_ar` across two families (`job_titles` is only UNIQUE(job_family_id, name_ar)), so those rows would be permanently unresolvable under name-only matching -- the optional family column disambiguates them, and a name still matching more than one row is skipped with an explicit per-row error rather than guessed at. All 58 real `org_units` names are distinct today, but the same ambiguity check is applied there anyway rather than assuming that holds. **[استنتاج] on idempotency**: `vacancies` has NO unique constraint at all (unlike `career_path`'s UNIQUE(from,to)), so re-importing the same file would silently duplicate everything -- (job_title_id, org_unit_id) among non-deleted rows is treated as the natural key, meaning a re-import updates the existing posting's status/requirements in place; an organization wanting two simultaneous postings for the same title in the same unit can't express that through this import. Duplicate pairs WITHIN one file are also rejected per-row rather than racing each other. Blank `الحالة` defaults to `'open'` (the DB default). Every write goes through the caller's own RLS-respecting client -- real authorization stays `vacancies_insert`'s `check_vpra('vacancies','approve', org_unit_id)` (hr_admin-only) and `vacancies_update`'s `'recommend'` bar, so an under-privileged caller's rows surface as per-row `الإدراج: ...` errors, never silent writes. Verified live end-to-end against the real shared database with a real temporary `hr_admin` test account, dev server on an explicit free port (3411) to avoid the documented cross-session port-3000 collision: the template downloaded (200, correct xlsx content-type, 8223 bytes), the submit button stayed disabled until a file was chosen, a 3-row file created exactly 2 real vacancies (blank status correctly stored as `'open'`) with the third row -- a deliberately nonexistent job title -- skipped with the exact expected Arabic error, and a matching `audit_log` row (`vacancies_excel_imported`, created:2/updated:0/rowErrorCount:1); the list re-rendered with the new rows without a manual reload (`router.refresh()`); re-importing a modified file for one of the same pairs reported "0 new, 1 updated" and the database confirmed the SAME row id updated in place (status open->closed, new requirements) with still exactly 3 rows total, not a duplicate. Separately confirmed the permission boundary is real, not just structural: a real temporary `employee`-role account could SELECT all 3 vacancies (its genuine `vacancies=view` grant) but its INSERT was rejected by Postgres with `42501`. Could not capture a screenshot this round -- the Browser pane wasn't displayed so it wasn't compositing frames (an environment limitation, reported rather than fabricated); everything above was verified via real DOM inspection and direct database queries. All test data (2 auth users/profiles/user_roles rows, 2 test vacancies, 2 audit rows, 2 temporary xlsx fixtures placed under `public/`) deleted afterward scoped to the exact created ids -- confirmed `vacancies` back to exactly the 1 real production row, untouched throughout. One incidental, non-blocking observation: `audit_log.actor_id` is `ON DELETE SET NULL`, so deleting a temporary auth user first nulls its audit rows' actor before an actor-scoped cleanup can match them -- delete audit rows by id, or before the user. `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (154 tests, unchanged -- a new Server Action plus a component copied from an already-tested pattern, no new pure function needing dedicated coverage) all passed clean.

- **New "التوظيف" (Recruitment) module -- 3 tabs + its own permissions section** (2026-08-04, migration `20260804000001_recruitment_process_area.sql`), per direct request ("اعمل على انشاء موديول باسم (التوظيف) وله عدة تابات أحدها اسمه خطة التوظيف والثاني اسمه الترقيات والثالث اسمه الشواغر (وهذا تضع فيه الجزء الجاهز من شواغر) ويكون له سكشن خاص في الصلاحيات باسم التوظيف"). Deliberately scaffolding only -- the project owner explicitly said each tab's design comes next ("بمجرد تنتهي سنبدأ بتصميم كل تاب لوحده"), so no `recruitment_plan` table was invented.

- **Only ONE new process area, not three**: `recruitmentPlan` (23rd) is genuinely new (nothing answers "can this person see/prepare the hiring plan"), while الترقيات/الشواغر REUSE the existing `promotions`/`vacancies` areas that already gate those real tables' RLS -- the same reuse-don't-duplicate precedent as `strategicPlanning` for strategic reports, `evaluation` for feedback_360, `promotions` for rewards. Because `processAreaSections` enforces one-section-per-area (existing test), `promotions`/`vacancies` MOVED out of "الموارد البشرية والمسار الوظيفي" into the new "التوظيف" section rather than being listed twice.

- **Known coupling, flagged rather than silently absorbed**: `promotions` also gates `rewards` and `recommendations`, so a grant made under the new التوظيف section reaches the whole "التوصيات" module too -- splitting them would need a second area plus rewrites of those tables' policies, a deliberate follow-up decision, not something to do implicitly inside a scaffolding PR. Zero `role_permissions` rows seeded for `recruitmentPlan` (CLAUDE.md §4-B least privilege, same precedent as `performanceReports`/`competencyReports`); `promotions`/`vacancies` grants were not touched at all, so no role gained or lost any capability (verified before/after: 10 and 8 rows respectively, unchanged). Nav: new `recruitment` group in `navGroups` (children `recruitment/plan`, `promotions`, `vacancies`), `vacancies` removed from the flat top-level `navItems` (7 -> 6 items; the page itself untouched), `GroupTabs`' groupKey union extended, and the tab bar added to all three member pages. New `/recruitment/plan` page is an honest gated placeholder: it checks `recruitmentPlan>=view` via the existing `get_my_permissions()` RPC and shows either a "قيد التصميم" card or a forbidden message. Verified live end-to-end against the real shared database with two real temporary accounts (dev server on an explicit free port, per the documented cross-session port-3000 hazard): a `super_admin` account -- which genuinely holds `promotions`/`vacancies` but NOT the unseeded `recruitmentPlan` -- saw the التوظيف group in the sidebar with only 2 of the 3 tabs, and got the real forbidden message on a direct `/recruitment/plan` visit (proving the gate is server-side, not a hidden link); a second account on a temporary custom role granted `recruitmentPlan`/`promotions`/`vacancies` at `view` saw all 3 tabs, the placeholder card, and correct active-tab highlighting on each of the three pages; `/admin`'s permission matrix rendered a new "التوظيف" section containing exactly خطة التوظيف / الترقيات والمكافآت / الشواغر with the test role's three `view` grants correctly checked. Hit the previously-documented stale-Turbopack-cache spurious 404 on the brand-new route (cleared `.next`, restarted -- not a code defect). All test data (2 auth users/profiles/user_roles rows, 1 custom role + its 3 `role_permissions` rows) deleted afterward scoped to the exact created ids -- confirmed zero residual rows, zero `recruitmentPlan` grants, and `promotions`/`vacancies` grant counts unchanged. `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint`, and the full `vitest run` suite (157 tests -- 3 new for the recruitment group's composition and visibility, several existing nav/process-area count assertions updated since they encoded the very structure being changed) all passed clean.

- **"خطة التوظيف" (Recruitment Plan) built -- the التوظيف module's first real tab** (2026-08-04, migration `20260804000002_recruitment_plans.sql`), replacing the gated placeholder shipped hours earlier.

- **Shape confirmed with the project owner before any code**: a proposal was presented grounded in what this database already holds, not a generic HR template, and approved verbatim ("نعم لكل المقترحات، والصلاحية لـ hr_admin اعتماد و super_admin اطلاع"). The grounding facts, checked by direct query first: `org_structure_positions` has 49 real positions but only 5 staffed via `org_structure_assignments` -> **44 genuinely vacant**, all 44 carrying a real `org_unit_id` and 41 of 44 a real `job_title_id`; `salary_scale` has 336 real rows keyed by job title. So the plan is built FROM the org chart and flows INTO `vacancies`, rather than being retyped by hand. Two tables: `recruitment_plans` (one plan per year -- partial `UNIQUE(plan_year) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL`, same NULL-safe pattern as `evaluations`) and `recruitment_plan_items` (one planned hire each: org unit, optional job title, optional link to the vacant chart position, headcount, target quarter 1-4, priority, estimated monthly cost, justification, status, and `vacancy_id` once published). `[استنتاج]` calls flagged in the migration header: statuses are plain TEXT (no CHECK enum, same precedent as `promotions.status`); `job_title_id` is NULLABLE *specifically because* 3 of the 44 real vacant positions genuinely have none, so NOT NULL would silently drop them from the import; `org_unit_id` IS NOT NULL (all 44 real ones have it, checked before deciding); `estimated_monthly_cost` is STORED not computed-on-read -- seeded from `salary_scale.step_a` but editable, so an approved plan keeps the number it was approved with if the scale is later revised. RLS uses `check_vpra_global('recruitmentPlan', ...)` (SELECT `view`, INSERT/UPDATE `prepare`, no DELETE policy -- soft-delete only) rather than the org-scoped `check_vpra()`: a recruitment plan is one university-wide document, and gating items per-unit while their parent plan is global would let a scoped role see totals it cannot reconcile from the items it may read -- flagged as a deliberate both-or-neither choice.
